# Technical research: one unified central Review Panel (run 2, report only)

## Task Overview

This task conducts a technical investigation of three overlapping review efforts and produces ONE structured research report at `.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RESEARCH-review-panel-run2-20261114-075454/RESEARCH-REPORT-review-panel-unification-run2.md`. The three efforts are: E1 (reflect Tier 2 panel kernel, shadow mode and the 5x3 model-combination experiment on branch `ironbelly/shadow-runs`), E2 (the tasklist review-panel study, collaboration designs and process-redesign brainstorms on `origin/ironbelly/eval-tasklist-review-panel` at tip `6c0984326cbb8fb06c4a36065de58b171d3f8175`), and E3 (the #346 review loop on master plus the installed-only experiment variants in `~/.claude/`).

GOAL (verbatim from the build request): Conduct a technical investigation of three overlapping review efforts (E1: reflect Tier 2 panel kernel, shadow mode and the model-combination experiment on branch `ironbelly/shadow-runs`; E2: the tasklist review-panel study, collaboration designs and process-redesign brainstorms on `origin/ironbelly/eval-tasklist-review-panel` at tip `6c0984326cbb8fb06c4a36065de58b171d3f8175`; E3: the #346 review loop on master plus the installed-only experiment variants in `~/.claude/`) and produce a structured research report that recommends ONE unified central Review Panel design: a shared core with thin modules for sc:reflect (Tier 2 shadow, then panel), sc:workflow and tasklist review, spec review from sc:brainstorm and sc:design, and later PR code review and rf-qa / rf-qa-qualitative replacement. The report must contain: (a) an overlap matrix across E1, E2, E3; (b) a value inventory in which every finding, lesson, strategy, defect, script, test, artifact and question is mapped to exactly one destination (core, a named module, evaluation, harness, knowledge, or DROP with the reason); (c) a conflict log in which conflicts the evidence cannot decide are resolved by `/sc:adversarial --compare` debates and conflicts the evidence does decide are decided without a debate; (d) a target architecture (shared core plus thin modules); (e) a future-use-case exposure check showing that the final shape does not block PR code review, rf-qa replacement or later reviewers.

WHY (summary): the owner wants all value from three overlapping efforts captured in one central Review Panel, with overlap evaluated and de-duplicated, conflicts resolved, and future use cases (PR code review, rf-qa replacement) not hindered by the final shape. The findings feed a later adversarial comparison and merge with a second, independent report, and then an implementation plan, so the report must be exhaustive (the anti-omission gate is mandatory), faithful to the code, and explicit about what the evidence does not show.

OWNER SCOPE CHANGE (binding): REPORT ONLY. No roadmap, branch plan, PR plan or risk register. Section 8 of the report holds the value inventory (8.1) and the sequence-and-gates inputs for a later plan (8.2: constraints and no-regression gates such as the byte-identical reflect contract 1.8.0 and the git ref constraint that `feat/review-panel` and `feat/review-panel/<name>` cannot both exist), not a roadmap. This task performs no implementation, merge, worktree creation, branch creation, commit or push. Testing requirements: not applicable (documentation-only research task; the only commands run are read-only recounts plus the scratch fixtures under `/tmp/review-panel-run2-<item>/` and the staging commands in Phase 7).

INDEPENDENCE (binding): this report stays independent of the first run's report. No agent reads, lists, greps into or cites `.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RESEARCH-review-panel-unify-20261113-155942/` or `.dev/tasks/to-do/REVIEW-PANEL-UNIFY-RUN2-QUEUED.md`. Repo-wide searches exclude `.dev/tasks/`; `git log` calls exclude it (`-- ':!.dev/tasks'`). One earlier slip is on record (scope slice 03 gap 10: file names printed, no content opened).

## Key Objectives

The following objectives MUST be achieved by this task:

1. **Verified topic findings:** 18 topic research files (`research/01..18-*.md`) and 6 value-ledger files (`research/19..24-*.md`), each claim tagged [CODE-VERIFIED], [CODE-CONTRADICTED] or [UNVERIFIED], with file:line evidence, produced in two waves because of the 20-concurrent-subagent limit.
2. **Gated research and synthesis:** research completeness gate (Phase 3) and synthesis quality gate (Phase 5), each with 15 lens agents (5 lens types partitioned over 3 file groups), serialized fix, verification, halt precedence regression then monotonicity then hard cap (2 fix cycles = 3 reviews).
3. **External context:** 4 web research files (`research/web-01..04-*.md`) on multi-model review evidence, Claude Code hooks and headless semantics, git branch hierarchy and stacked PRs, and finding schemas and review bots.
4. **Conflict adjudication:** every conflict in `synthesis/synth-06-conflict-log.md` that the evidence cannot decide is resolved by a `/sc:adversarial --compare` debate under `reviews/adversarial/<conflict-slug>/`; conflicts the evidence decides are decided without a debate; winners and unaddressed invariants are folded back into synth-06, synth-07 and synth-10.
5. **The deliverable:** `RESEARCH-REPORT-review-panel-unification-run2.md` assembled by a single rf-assembler from 10 synthesis files, containing (a) overlap matrix, (b) value inventory with exactly one destination per item, (c) conflict log, (d) target architecture, (e) future-use exposure check, and Section 8.2 inputs for a later plan.
6. **Final-report QA:** Gate 3 lens validation (13 lenses, two instances each when the report exceeds 3000 lines), Gate 4 source-document fidelity (4 agents plus a cross-source contradiction agent), Gate 5 anti-omission (4 rf-analyst, exhaustive, by meaning), and a final validation checklist.
7. **Close-out:** summary to the user, `.dev/tasks` staged (no commit, no push), next steps recorded in the Task Log.

### Phase 2: Deep Investigation (L1 Discovery, two waves because of the 20-subagent limit)
(Refer to the BUILD-REQUEST "Phase 2 — Deep Investigation" and research-notes.md SUGGESTED_PHASES for detailed requirements)

Phase 2 has 24 agents and the harness refuses more than 20 concurrent subagents. Wave 1 = items 2.01-2.18 (18 agents, spawned together in ONE message). Wave 2 = items 2.19-2.24 (6 value-ledger agents, spawned together in ONE message when slots free, i.e. after at least 6 wave-1 agents have returned). NEVER spawn all 24 at once. Every agent writes its own output file incrementally; if context limits are reached, the output files persist on disk and unchecked items are resumed in the next session.

**Step 2.1:** Wave 1 topic agents (items 2.01-2.18, spawn in parallel in one message)

- [x] (Item 2.01, wave 1) Spawn ONE Agent (subagent_type `rf-task-researcher`, investigation type Architecture Analyst) to build the overlap capability matrix across E1, E2 and E3, passing it the quoted prompt below with the ASSIGNMENT, so that Section 2.9 of the report (overlap matrix) rests on code-verified cells. AGENT PROMPT (pass verbatim): "Investigate the topic below for the research question 'which ONE unified central Review Panel design (shared core plus thin modules) captures all the value of E1 (reflect Tier 2 panel kernel, shadow mode, model-combination experiment), E2 (tasklist review-panel study, collaboration designs, process-redesign brainstorms) and E3 (the #346 review loop and installed-only variants), and keeps future PR code review and rf-qa replacement unblocked' and write your findings to the output path. INCREMENTAL FILE WRITING PROTOCOL (violation causes data loss): (1) your FIRST ACTION is to create the output file with the header `# Research: <topic>`, `**Investigation type:**`, `**Scope:**`, `**Status:** In Progress`, `**Date:**` and a `---` rule; (2) after investigating each file, component or logical unit IMMEDIATELY append your findings with Edit and never accumulate findings in your context; (3) never rewrite the file from scratch; (4) when finished change Status to Complete and append the Summary. RESEARCH PROTOCOL: read actual source, trace data flow, document key classes and functions with file paths and line numbers, identify patterns, check edge cases and fallbacks, note dependencies in both directions, flag gaps, note integration opportunities. DOCUMENTATION STALENESS PROTOCOL: documentation describes intent or history while code describes the current state, so cross-validate every structural claim taken from a document (components, pipelines, call chains, file paths, counts, line numbers) against the code before reporting it as current, and mark every doc-sourced claim [CODE-VERIFIED] (confirmed at file:line), [CODE-CONTRADICTED] (say what the code shows) or [UNVERIFIED] (no corresponding code found); [UNVERIFIED] and [CODE-CONTRADICTED] claims must appear in Gaps and Questions; a document saying X exists does not verify that X exists. OUTPUT FORMAT: descriptive headers per unit, actual file paths, class and function names and line numbers, anomalies flagged, **[STALE DOC]** markers, a Key Takeaways bullet list after each section, and at the end the sections `## Gaps and Questions`, `## Stale Documentation Found` and `## Summary` (3-5 sentences). Be thorough and specific; document only what you verified in the source. ### Phase 3: Research Completeness Verification (Gate 1, full intensity, 15 lens agents, serialized fix)
(Refer to the BUILD-REQUEST "Phase 3" and Template 02 I15, I16, I19, I20, M3 for detailed requirements)

Gate 1 spawns 15 report-only agents (5 lens types, each partitioned over three file groups because more than 6 research files exist): partition A = research files 01-08, partition B = 09-16, partition C = 17-24. All 15 are spawned in ONE message (15 is below the 20-agent limit). Cycle control (halt precedence, strictly in this order): (1) REGRESSION: if any item that passed at the previous review now fails, HALT with the message `Regression detected on Item X.Y, previously PASS at cycle N, now FAIL. Halt overrides monotonicity check.` (X.Y and N replaced by the actual item and cycle, all other characters byte-exact); (2) MONOTONICITY: if the number of open blocking findings does not fall (|F_{n+1}| >= |F_n|), HALT with `[HALT-MONOTONICITY] |F|=<n>` (n replaced by the count); (3) HARD CAP: 2 fix cycles = 3 reviews, then HALT and escalate to the user with the open blocking findings; (4) otherwise proceed. Minor findings are notes and never fail a review or trigger a fix pass. The counters of different gates are independent.

**Step 5.5:** Conflict adjudication (after Gate 2 passed, BEFORE assembly; `/sc:adversarial --compare` only, never `--merge`)

Every conflict in `synthesis/synth-06-conflict-log.md` that the evidence cannot decide gets one debate under `reviews/adversarial/<conflict-slug>/`; conflicts the evidence does decide stay decided in synth-06 without a debate. Debates run in four batches of three slots (a debate spawns several agents of its own, so a batch never exceeds three debates, and the next batch starts only after every item of the previous batch is complete). Proceed only if `qa/gate2-cycle-control.md` records a PASS.

### Task Summary

**Completion Date:** 2026-11-14 (started 2026-11-14 10:37, completed 23:36 by the Execution Log times; about 13 hours elapsed)

**Work Completed:**
- Final report: `.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RESEARCH-review-panel-run2-20261114-075454/RESEARCH-REPORT-review-panel-unification-run2.md` (5,098 lines, sha256 `b3195608a09aa4c9d069dd4779e8b8e04284bc975cbaa84f1e90d4b56ce50775`). Report only: no roadmap, plan or implementation.
- Counts (Glob and shell counts): 24 research files, 4 web files, 12 scope slices, 10 synthesis files (3.24 MB; the value inventory `synth-08` has 5,950 rows), 139 QA files in `qa/`, 15 debate folders under `reviews/adversarial/` (each with `ADJUDICATION-RESULT.md`), 499 `.md` files in the task folder in total; 534 files of the task folder are staged.
- Recommendation in one sentence: one shared Review Panel core in `src/superclaude/cli/review/`, built as a thin shared contract (one finding id grammar, one limits module, one repair step, one reference-token list, tier-file panel resolution, a run record, a dispatch contract) with per-module engines side by side (spec review on the v1 anchor engine; reflect and later PR review on the v2 citation engine), and thin consumer modules made of profile data, brief blocks and an output adapter.
- Total phases: 7. Checklist items: 190.
- Key outputs: the final report (above); `phase-outputs/reports/final-summary.md` (the summary presented to the owner); `qa/final-validation-checklist.md`; `phase-outputs/discovery/undecidable-conflicts.md`; `phase-outputs/reports/adjudication-fold-report.md`; `synthesis/synth-08-value-inventory.md` (Appendix A of the report); `gaps-and-questions.md`; `reviews/adversarial/<slug>/` (15 debates).
- QA gate outcomes (reviews used, fix cycles, verdict):
  - Gate 1 (research files, 15 lens reports): 3 reviews, 2 fix cycles (`F_0 = 11`, `F_1 = 2`, `F_2 = 0`); PASS at review 3. Three findings were carried to item 5.08 by owner decision and one was downgraded.
  - Gate 2 (synthesis files, 15 lens reports): 2 reviews, 1 fix cycle (`F_0 = 8`, `F_1 = 0`); PASS at review 2. Owner decision A (stricter blocking rule, `qa/grading-override.md`) applied from here on.
  - Debates: 15 adversarial debates (55 routed conflict rows), all with an `ADJUDICATION-RESULT.md`; folded into the synthesis and the report (`phase-outputs/reports/adjudication-fold-report.md`).
  - Gate 3 (report lenses, split mode, 26 lens reports): 2 reviews, 1 fix cycle (`F_0 = 2`, `F_1 = 0`); PASS at review 2.
  - Gate 4 (fidelity to sources, 4 agents plus 1 cross-source agent): 1 review, 0 fix cycles (`F_0 = 0`); PASS at review 2 by the control rule.
  - Gate 5 (anti-omission, 4 agents, partition moved 5 ledger files, one agent re-run closed the recount finding `P6A-RC-1`): 1 review, 0 fix cycles (`F_0 = 0`); PASS at review 2 by the control rule.
  - Final validation (23 checklist items): PASS, 0 blocking, no fix.

**Challenges Encountered:**
- Three debate orchestrators (C-01, C-02, C-12) stopped with "waiting" and needed a nudge to run their agent calls in the foreground.
- The 15 debate names differed between two synthesis files (15 against 14 markers); fixed in the Gate 2 fix.
- One synthesis row was missing from `synth-10` and was added by the orchestrator.
- The context window was compacted more than once, so progress was re-read from this file each time.
- The edit freshness hook blocked several edits until the file was read first.
- Two independence slips: a Gate 2 agent saw the run-1 queue file name and snippets of seven other task-file lines (not used), and slice 03 earlier; both were disclosed.
- Reading 26 lens reports and the large ledgers exceeded what one context could hold, so consolidations were delegated (see Deviations).

**Deviations from Process:**
- Agent prompts were delivered by task-file line reference (the agent reads its own quoted prompt from its item line) instead of being pasted into each spawn call; content is the same bytes.
- No separate generic 6-agent phase-gate QA after some phases; the task file's own Gates 1 to 5 serve as the gates for those files.
- Owner decision A (grading override, `qa/grading-override.md`) replaced the blocking definition inside the gate items from Gate 2 onward, for this task only.
- The debates ran through orchestrator agents in parallel with merged batches; the axis 6.10 was not debated.
- Consolidation items 6.29, 6.44 and 6.58, the Gate 5 partition work and the Step 7.1 summary draft were done by delegated agents (rf-analyst or rf-qa) instead of the executor reading every report itself, because the reports exceeded the executor's context; the executor then read the file heads and verdict lines and the cited counts, and verified the results on disk.
- For Gate 5 the partition check moved five ledger files (see `qa/gate5-partition.md`); moved ledgers carried their row-recount and pair-check rules with them via the spawn wrapper.
- Item 6.38 was ticked before its append to `qa/gate3-cycle-control.md` had succeeded (the edit was blocked by the freshness hook); the append was made straight afterwards.
- Gate 4 and Gate 5 cycle-2 and fix items wrote skip notes because their control files recorded PASS at review 2.
- Final-state check: no repeated lens QA run on the final text (I17 items 5 and 6); replaced by the hash comparison recorded in the Execution Log. The report text is the same content that Gate 3 verification 1 passed, because only Gate 3 fix cycle 1 edited it.

### Builder Notes (recorded by the task builder at creation, 2026-11-14)

- **Template and tier:** Template 02, QA intensity full, report only (owner scope change). 7 phases, 12 scope slices as inputs. Phase 2 has 24 items in two waves (items 2.01-2.18 and 2.19-2.24) because of the 20-concurrent-subagent limit; no item spawns all 24.
- **Gate partitions chosen by the builder:** Gate 1 (15 agents: 5 lens types x 3 partitions of 8 research files), Gate 2 (15 agents: 5 lens types x 3 partitions of synthesis files), Gate 3 (13 lenses: 5 rf-qa structural, 5 rf-qa-qualitative content, 3 domain; each lens has an instance a item and an instance b item that is used only when the report exceeds 3000 lines, decided by Item 6.02), Gate 4 (4 fidelity agents plus 1 cross-source contradiction agent per M4 Step 3), Gate 5 (4 rf-analyst anti-omission agents). Every cycle is encoded as explicit items: consolidation, ONE fix agent, structural and content verification, control item with halt precedence regression, monotonicity, hard cap (2 fix cycles = 3 reviews); cycle 2 items skip themselves when cycle 1 passed.
- **Adjudication slots:** the conflicts the evidence cannot decide are unknown at build time, so Item 5.37 enumerates them from synth-06 into 12 fixed slots (C-01 to C-12, items 5.38-5.49, in four batches of three) plus a DYNAMIC CONTENT block (`extra-debates`) for slots beyond C-12; unused slots write a not-used note. This is why the frontmatter says `task_type: dynamic`. Item 5.50 folds the results into synth-06, synth-07 (it replaces every `[PENDING-ADJUDICATION: <slug>]` marker that synth-07 may carry by design), synth-09 and synth-10.
- **Deviation note (I17 items 5 and 6):** Post-Completion does not repeat a full lens QA run or a source-fidelity validation on the final report; Gate 3 (lens QA), Gate 4 (source-document fidelity), Gate 5 (anti-omission) and the final validation (Items 6.68-6.70) run in Phase 6 instead, each with its own fix cycles. The fix agents of Gate 4 (Items 6.45, 6.50), Gate 5 (Items 6.59, 6.64) and the final validation (Item 6.69) can edit the report after Gate 3 and Gate 4 reviewed it, so Gate 3 and Gate 4 reviewed earlier revisions plus the verification rounds after each of their own fixes, not necessarily the final text; only the last verification round (Item 6.68, or its re-check in Item 6.70 when a fix pass ran) can have passed on the content whose sha256 Item 6.70 records in the Task Log, and Item 6.70 records whether that round's own hash equals it. The Post-Completion final-state check compares the current report sha256 with that recorded value and HALTs if they differ. The deviation is also recorded in the Task Log under ### Deviations from Process.
- **Deviation note (Phase 6 final validation):** Items 6.68-6.70 allow ONE fix pass and one re-check for the final checklist (the build request says every blocking finding is resolved before Phase 7); a blocking FAIL after that pass halts the task.
- **Deviation note (Gate 3 size handling):** the build request lists 3-5 rf-qa plus 3-5 rf-qa-qualitative lenses scaled by size (I19); the builder encoded the top of that range (5 + 5 + 3 domain = 13) because the report will exceed 1500 lines.
- **Documentation staleness:** no item builds on a [CODE-CONTRADICTED] or [UNVERIFIED] claim of research-notes.md AMBIGUITIES_FOR_USER; Phase 2 items 2.01 (open item d), 2.04 (e), 2.05 (e), 2.08 (b, d and e), 2.10, 2.11 and 2.17 (e) name the contradicted claims explicitly and instruct the agent to verify and correct them; between them they own all 13 [CODE-CONTRADICTED] rows and the numeric [UNVERIFIED] disagreements (combos/ size, RESULT.md count, quarantine count, Qwen reviewer count) of research-notes.md AMBIGUITIES_FOR_USER.
- **Evidence-absence markers:** items whose subject has no known code line at build time (E1 artifacts, E2 branch material, ledgers, web research, E3 archive, PR-review call sites) carry an `<!-- evidence-absence: ... -->` comment; items with known anchors carry them inline (for example `findings.py:29`, `panel.py:80-136`, `.gitignore:13`).
- **Outward action not covered by the owner's instruction:** committing or pushing run 2. Phase 7 only stages `.dev/tasks` and records the next steps; it does not commit or push.
- **Web provenance:** the web research items name Tavily first (`mcp__litellm__Tavily-tavily_search` and `-extract`, else `mcp__tavily__tavily_search` and `-extract`) with WebSearch and WebFetch as a fallback that must be annotated with `web-provenance`.
